Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: User wants to review their session for learning opportunities. user: "Analyze this session to improve future sessions" assistant: "I'll use the session-analyzer agent to identify patterns and improvement opportunities." &lt;commentary&gt; Session analyzer finds workflow patterns and friction points from session metrics. &lt;… ([HarmAalbers/claude-requirements-framework](https://github.com/HarmAalbers/claude-requirements-framework/blob/HEAD/plugins/requirements-framework/agents/session-analyzer.md) · ★2)
- **session-analyzer**: Use this agent when running retrospective analysis, extracting session learnings, or analyzing mistakes and decisions. ([FrancisVarga/coconut-claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/FrancisVarga/coconut-claude-code-plugins/blob/HEAD/coconut-rules/agents/session-analyzer.md) · ★1)
- **session-analyzer**: Core agent that analyses current session conversation to generate Harvest data. ([lledellebell/glean](https://github.com/lledellebell/glean/blob/HEAD/agents/session-analyzer.md) · ★1)
- **session-analyzer**: Analyzes Claude Code session JSONL files to find repeating patterns, workarounds, and tribal knowledge across sessions in a project. Returns structured JSON with skill candidates ranked by score. Use for session history mining and skill candidate discovery. ([materkey/cc-plugins](https://github.com/materkey/cc-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/skill-workshop/agents/session-analyzer.md) · ★1)
- **session-analyzer**: docs/questions.md를 분석해서 반복 패턴·병목·개선안을 docs/insights.md에 작성 ([FineHourTeam/ansimroad](https://github.com/FineHourTeam/ansimroad/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-analyzer.md))
- **session-architect**: Session and container management specialist for Autonomic. Knows Claude Code CLI flags, stream-json output parsing, container lifecycle (Podman/Docker), completion detection, cost tracking, rate budget, and worktree isolation. Dispatch for session management work. ([Sephyi/autonomic](https://github.com/Sephyi/autonomic/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-architect.md))

- **session-archiver**: 子セッション 1 本の archive_session を、まず打ち・拒否されたときだけ待って再試行する専用のエージェント。親 (監督役) が基準 3 点 (PR MERGED / 掃除 / 未消化の申し送り無し) を判定したあと background で起動する。1 回目は待たずに archive_session を打ち、拒否された場合だけ isRunning:false が 2 回続き lastActivityAt が 60 秒以上前になるまで待ってから再試行する (実質 2 回まで — 2 回目も拒否されると hook が pending を立てて待てなくなるため、そこで中断して返す)。子への send_message・self-archive の依頼・worktree/branch の削除・PR の判定はしない。 ([ippoan/claude-skills](https://github.com/ippoan/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-archiver.md))
- **session-archivist**: Keeps the root sessions ledger clean, complete, and promotive rather than letting useful chat history die in logs. ([starmynd-org/infinite-brain-os](https://github.com/starmynd-org/infinite-brain-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-archivist.md) · ★301 · 3 copies)
- **session-archivist**: Summarize archived session behavior and extract reusable lessons for future harness turns ([iLearn-Lab/EvoHarness](https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/EvoHarness/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-lab/agents/session-archivist.md) · ★60)
- **session-archivist**: Writes a durable post-merge session note for a shipped ticket into the engineer's state dir. Use right after a PR merges, before the engineer compacts. Returns the exact path of the note it wrote. Captures only; never touches code, branches, or the mailbox. - **session-auditor**: USE WHEN: 用户调 `/session-review` 想看当前 session 工作流执行情况(flow 推进 / agent 边界 / blocker 处理)。OUTPUT: 审查报告(只读) 或 flow 配置修复(`--fix`)。DO NOT USE: 修业务代码(只改 flow 配置) / 跨 session 趋势审查(走 workflow-reviewer) / 单纯查 task 状态(直接读 task.json)。 ([dd-xiaozhi/dev-agent-flow](https://github.com/dd-xiaozhi/dev-agent-flow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **session-auditor**: Orchestrates one Claude Code session's audit end to end -- runs the cost and timeline extractors, fans out to 5 general-purpose shards (4 parallel, 1 sequential), merges their digests, and renders the self-contained audit_session-&lt;sid&gt;.html. ([brunogsa/unix-utils](https://github.com/brunogsa/unix-utils/blob/HEAD/configs/ai-docs/claude/agents/session-auditor.md))
- **session-auditor**: Reviews what changed in a session and checks against CLAUDE.md rules ([Khaledaun/Yalla-london](https://github.com/Khaledaun/Yalla-london/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-auditor.md))
- **session-auditor**: Session audit critic; dispatched with ROLE=bug_hunt or ROLE=claim_bust. Read-only adversarial lens on invoking-thread deliverables. Returns structured findings only — no writes, no fixes. ([mrkinglollipop/manna-links](https://github.com/mrkinglollipop/manna-links/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/session-auditor.md))
- **session-auditor**: Audits a Claude Code session transcript for token spend and harness-usage quality — wasted calls, missed delegation, cache misses — and writes a graded retrospective. Use after a session ends. ([prototypdigital/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/prototypdigital/claude-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/backend/agents/session-auditor.md) · 10 copies)
- **session-auditor**: Audit Pi SDK session storage for unbounded growth, missing TTLs, and cross-tenant data leaks. ([srbryers/Yumi](https://github.com/srbryers/Yumi/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-auditor.md))
- **session-autostart-2026-10-03**: Lane: `dotfiles-20261003T103159.259690000-05.session-autostart`, branch `feat/session-autostart-no-prompts`. Engine `claude --version` → 2.1.288. `$CC` = `~/dev/github/ray-manaloto/knowledge-base/sources/agent-harness-docs/docs/claude-code`. Companion: `claude-code-expert-autostart-stall-review-2026-10-03.md` (binary-level read; its "requester unattributed" for the coordinator kill is closed below ([ray-manaloto/dotfiles-legacy](https://github.com/ray-manaloto/dotfiles-legacy/blob/HEAD/docs/research/kb/reports/agents/session-autostart-2026-10-03.md) · ★5)
- **session-boundary-agent**: 判断是否到达 session 边界（该 checkpoint/compact/clear/branch/换 reviewer）并维护 session-tree 时使用。 ([kanydao/ml-template](https://github.com/kanydao/ml-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-boundary-agent.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **session-bridge**: Cross-session context persistence — auto-saves decisions, conventions, progress, instincts to .rune/ files. Loads + integrity-checks at session start. Auto-triggered before compaction. ([Rune-kit/rune](https://github.com/Rune-kit/rune/blob/HEAD/agents/session-bridge.md) · ★93)
- **session-bridge-agent**: Use when a subagent should save or load cross-session state in .topia/ (decisions, conventions, progress), for example before compaction or at session start. Runs the session-bridge skill's steps. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/agents/session-bridge-agent.md) · ★1)
- **session-brief**: What every agent session on this project needs to know. A session prompt names only the issue and what is specific to it; the rest is here. Shared rules live in the workflow kit and are linked, not copied. ([DcBexter/MetaboliteDetector](https://github.com/DcBexter/MetaboliteDetector/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/session-brief.md))
- **session-brief**: Reads process docs, architecture docs, active plans, and the last commit to produce a concise session brief. Run at the start of any session to get up to speed without re-exploring the codebase. - **session-budget**: Session length is governed by CONTEXT SIZE, never by a task count. Re-reading a long context is the orchestrator's largest cost, and a session that fills mid-task is the hardest one to stop. So plan the work so that you are always one **clean logical break** away from stopping: all work committed, the tracker current, no sub-agent running. ([Zenosyne-Technologies/agent-operating-kit](https://github.com/Zenosyne-Technologies/agent-operating-kit/blob/HEAD/templates/marvin/agents/session-budget.md))

- **session-protocol**: 1. Read root `AGENTS.md` — you're doing it now. 2. Run `bd ready` to see available work. 3. Before editing any module, read its local `AGENTS.md` (or `CLAUDE.md` as fallback). 4. Verify which repo you're in: `git rev-parse --show-toplevel`. 5. When done: run the Session Close Protocol below: `bd backup`, commit, `bd orphans`, `bd backup`, `bash .beads/push.sh`, then `git push`.
